c﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍﻿ompute the Hurst exponent:

T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿he event series is persistent (trending). Events cluster: periods of rapid change follow periods of rapid change,
a﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿nd periods of stability follow stability. This is the same pattern observed in the social media data (H = 0.931) and
t﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿he academic transcript data (H = 0.915).

F﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿ractal self-similarity means the same patterns repeat at different scales. In Islamic history, we observe:
P﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍﻿attern: Expansion → Peak → Contraction → Reset

S﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿cale
M﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿icro (decades)

M﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿eso (centuries)
